/*
    CoffeeRuu - Initial SQL Server Database
    Target: SQL Server 2019+ / SQL Server Express
    ORM: Entity Framework Core

    WARNING:
    - This script clears ALL data every time it is executed.
    - It preserves the database schema (tables, constraints, indexes).
    - It resets identity counters and inserts baseline data.
    - Do not run against a database containing data you need to keep.
    - Never store plain-text passwords. Store a secure password hash generated by the application.
*/

IF DB_ID(N'CoffeeRuuDB') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE DATABASE CoffeeRuuDB;
END;
GO

USE CoffeeRuuDB;
GO

/* 1. Authentication and authorization */
IF OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.Roles', N'U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.Roles
    (
        RoleId       INT IDENTITY(1,1) CONSTRAINT PK_Roles PRIMARY KEY,
        RoleName     NVARCHAR(50) NOT NULL CONSTRAINT UQ_Roles_RoleName UNIQUE,
        Description  NVARCHAR(250) NULL
    );
END;
GO

IF OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.Employees', N'U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.Employees
    (
        EmployeeId       INT IDENTITY(1,1) CONSTRAINT PK_Employees PRIMARY KEY,
        RoleId           INT NOT NULL,
        Username         NVARCHAR(50) NOT NULL CONSTRAINT UQ_Employees_Username UNIQUE,
        PasswordHash     NVARCHAR(500) NOT NULL,
        FullName         NVARCHAR(120) NOT NULL,
        Phone            NVARCHAR(20) NULL,
        Email            NVARCHAR(150) NULL,
        IsActive         BIT NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_Employees_IsActive DEFAULT (1),
        CreatedAt        DATETIME2(0) NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_Employees_CreatedAt DEFAULT (SYSDATETIME()),
        UpdatedAt        DATETIME2(0) NULL,
        CONSTRAINT FK_Employees_Roles FOREIGN KEY (RoleId) REFERENCES dbo.Roles(RoleId)
    );
END;
GO

/* 2. Menu, product options, ingredients and recipes */
IF OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.Categories', N'U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.Categories
    (
        CategoryId    INT IDENTITY(1,1) CONSTRAINT PK_Categories PRIMARY KEY,
        CategoryName  NVARCHAR(100) NOT NULL CONSTRAINT UQ_Categories_CategoryName UNIQUE,
        Description   NVARCHAR(250) NULL,
        IsActive      BIT NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_Categories_IsActive DEFAULT (1)
    );
END;
GO

IF OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.Products', N'U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.Products
    (
        ProductId     INT IDENTITY(1,1) CONSTRAINT PK_Products PRIMARY KEY,
        CategoryId    INT NOT NULL,
        ProductName   NVARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,
        Description   NVARCHAR(500) NULL,
        ImageUrl      NVARCHAR(500) NULL,
        IsAvailable   BIT NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_Products_IsAvailable DEFAULT (1),
        CreatedAt     DATETIME2(0) NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_Products_CreatedAt DEFAULT (SYSDATETIME()),
        CONSTRAINT FK_Products_Categories FOREIGN KEY (CategoryId) REFERENCES dbo.Categories(CategoryId)
    );
END;
GO

IF NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM sys.indexes
    WHERE name = N'IX_Products_CategoryId'
      AND object_id = OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.Products')
)
BEGIN
    CREATE INDEX IX_Products_CategoryId ON dbo.Products(CategoryId);
END;
GO

IF OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.ProductSizes', N'U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.ProductSizes
    (
        ProductSizeId  INT IDENTITY(1,1) CONSTRAINT PK_ProductSizes PRIMARY KEY,
        ProductId      INT NOT NULL,
        SizeName       NVARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,
        Price          DECIMAL(12,2) NOT NULL,
        IsAvailable    BIT NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_ProductSizes_IsAvailable DEFAULT (1),
        CONSTRAINT FK_ProductSizes_Products FOREIGN KEY (ProductId) REFERENCES dbo.Products(ProductId),
        CONSTRAINT UQ_ProductSizes_Product_Size UNIQUE (ProductId, SizeName),
        CONSTRAINT CK_ProductSizes_Price CHECK (Price >= 0)
    );
END;
GO

IF OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.Toppings', N'U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.Toppings
    (
        ToppingId     INT IDENTITY(1,1) CONSTRAINT PK_Toppings PRIMARY KEY,
        ToppingName   NVARCHAR(100) NOT NULL CONSTRAINT UQ_Toppings_ToppingName UNIQUE,
        ExtraPrice    DECIMAL(12,2) NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_Toppings_ExtraPrice DEFAULT (0),
        IsAvailable   BIT NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_Toppings_IsAvailable DEFAULT (1),
        CONSTRAINT CK_Toppings_ExtraPrice CHECK (ExtraPrice >= 0)
    );
END;
GO

IF OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.Ingredients', N'U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.Ingredients
    (
        IngredientId    INT IDENTITY(1,1) CONSTRAINT PK_Ingredients PRIMARY KEY,
        IngredientName  NVARCHAR(120) NOT NULL CONSTRAINT UQ_Ingredients_IngredientName UNIQUE,
        Unit            NVARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,
        CurrentStock    DECIMAL(12,3) NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_Ingredients_CurrentStock DEFAULT (0),
        MinimumStock    DECIMAL(12,3) NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_Ingredients_MinimumStock DEFAULT (0),
        IsActive        BIT NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_Ingredients_IsActive DEFAULT (1),
        CONSTRAINT CK_Ingredients_CurrentStock CHECK (CurrentStock >= 0),
        CONSTRAINT CK_Ingredients_MinimumStock CHECK (MinimumStock >= 0)
    );
END;
GO

IF OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.ProductRecipes', N'U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.ProductRecipes
    (
        ProductSizeId  INT NOT NULL,
        IngredientId   INT NOT NULL,
        QuantityUsed   DECIMAL(12,3) NOT NULL,
        CONSTRAINT PK_ProductRecipes PRIMARY KEY (ProductSizeId, IngredientId),
        CONSTRAINT FK_ProductRecipes_ProductSizes FOREIGN KEY (ProductSizeId) REFERENCES dbo.ProductSizes(ProductSizeId),
        CONSTRAINT FK_ProductRecipes_Ingredients FOREIGN KEY (IngredientId) REFERENCES dbo.Ingredients(IngredientId),
        CONSTRAINT CK_ProductRecipes_QuantityUsed CHECK (QuantityUsed > 0)
    );
END;
GO

IF OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.ToppingRecipes', N'U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.ToppingRecipes
    (
        ToppingId      INT NOT NULL,
        IngredientId   INT NOT NULL,
        QuantityUsed   DECIMAL(12,3) NOT NULL,
        CONSTRAINT PK_ToppingRecipes PRIMARY KEY (ToppingId, IngredientId),
        CONSTRAINT FK_ToppingRecipes_Toppings FOREIGN KEY (ToppingId) REFERENCES dbo.Toppings(ToppingId),
        CONSTRAINT FK_ToppingRecipes_Ingredients FOREIGN KEY (IngredientId) REFERENCES dbo.Ingredients(IngredientId),
        CONSTRAINT CK_ToppingRecipes_QuantityUsed CHECK (QuantityUsed > 0)
    );
END;
GO

/* 3. Suppliers and stock receiving */
IF OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.Suppliers', N'U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.Suppliers
    (
        SupplierId    INT IDENTITY(1,1) CONSTRAINT PK_Suppliers PRIMARY KEY,
        SupplierName  NVARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,
        Phone         NVARCHAR(20) NULL,
        Email         NVARCHAR(150) NULL,
        Address       NVARCHAR(300) NULL,
        IsActive      BIT NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_Suppliers_IsActive DEFAULT (1)
    );
END;
GO

IF OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.StockReceipts', N'U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.StockReceipts
    (
        StockReceiptId  INT IDENTITY(1,1) CONSTRAINT PK_StockReceipts PRIMARY KEY,
        SupplierId      INT NOT NULL,
        ReceivedBy      INT NOT NULL,
        ReceiptDate     DATETIME2(0) NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_StockReceipts_ReceiptDate DEFAULT (SYSDATETIME()),
        Note            NVARCHAR(500) NULL,
        TotalCost       DECIMAL(14,2) NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_StockReceipts_TotalCost DEFAULT (0),
        CONSTRAINT FK_StockReceipts_Suppliers FOREIGN KEY (SupplierId) REFERENCES dbo.Suppliers(SupplierId),
        CONSTRAINT FK_StockReceipts_Employees FOREIGN KEY (ReceivedBy) REFERENCES dbo.Employees(EmployeeId),
        CONSTRAINT CK_StockReceipts_TotalCost CHECK (TotalCost >= 0)
    );
END;
GO

IF OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.StockReceiptDetails', N'U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.StockReceiptDetails
    (
        StockReceiptDetailId  INT IDENTITY(1,1) CONSTRAINT PK_StockReceiptDetails PRIMARY KEY,
        StockReceiptId        INT NOT NULL,
        IngredientId          INT NOT NULL,
        Quantity              DECIMAL(12,3) NOT NULL,
        UnitCost              DECIMAL(12,2) NOT NULL,
        CONSTRAINT FK_StockReceiptDetails_Receipts FOREIGN KEY (StockReceiptId) REFERENCES dbo.StockReceipts(StockReceiptId),
        CONSTRAINT FK_StockReceiptDetails_Ingredients FOREIGN KEY (IngredientId) REFERENCES dbo.Ingredients(IngredientId),
        CONSTRAINT CK_StockReceiptDetails_Quantity CHECK (Quantity > 0),
        CONSTRAINT CK_StockReceiptDetails_UnitCost CHECK (UnitCost >= 0)
    );
END;
GO

IF OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.InventoryTransactions', N'U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.InventoryTransactions
    (
        InventoryTransactionId  BIGINT IDENTITY(1,1) CONSTRAINT PK_InventoryTransactions PRIMARY KEY,
        IngredientId            INT NOT NULL,
        EmployeeId              INT NULL,
        TransactionType         NVARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,
        QuantityChange          DECIMAL(12,3) NOT NULL,
        ReferenceType           NVARCHAR(40) NULL,
        ReferenceId             INT NULL,
        CreatedAt               DATETIME2(0) NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_InventoryTransactions_CreatedAt DEFAULT (SYSDATETIME()),
        Note                    NVARCHAR(300) NULL,
        CONSTRAINT FK_InventoryTransactions_Ingredients FOREIGN KEY (IngredientId) REFERENCES dbo.Ingredients(IngredientId),
        CONSTRAINT FK_InventoryTransactions_Employees FOREIGN KEY (EmployeeId) REFERENCES dbo.Employees(EmployeeId),
        CONSTRAINT CK_InventoryTransactions_Type CHECK (TransactionType IN (N'StockIn', N'SaleDeduction', N'Adjustment', N'Reversal')),
        CONSTRAINT CK_InventoryTransactions_Quantity CHECK (QuantityChange <> 0)
    );
END;
GO

IF NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM sys.indexes
    WHERE name = N'IX_InventoryTransactions_Ingredient_Date'
      AND object_id = OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.InventoryTransactions')
)
BEGIN
    CREATE INDEX IX_InventoryTransactions_Ingredient_Date ON dbo.InventoryTransactions(IngredientId, CreatedAt);
END;
GO

/* 4. Tables and reservations */
IF OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.CafeTables', N'U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.CafeTables
    (
        TableId       INT IDENTITY(1,1) CONSTRAINT PK_CafeTables PRIMARY KEY,
        TableName     NVARCHAR(50) NOT NULL CONSTRAINT UQ_CafeTables_TableName UNIQUE,
        Capacity      INT NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_CafeTables_Capacity DEFAULT (4),
        TableStatus   NVARCHAR(20) NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_CafeTables_TableStatus DEFAULT (N'Available'),
        IsActive      BIT NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_CafeTables_IsActive DEFAULT (1),
        CONSTRAINT CK_CafeTables_Capacity CHECK (Capacity > 0),
        CONSTRAINT CK_CafeTables_Status CHECK (TableStatus IN (N'Available', N'Occupied', N'Reserved', N'OutOfService'))
    );
END;
GO

IF OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.TableReservations', N'U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.TableReservations
    (
        ReservationId   INT IDENTITY(1,1) CONSTRAINT PK_TableReservations PRIMARY KEY,
        TableId         INT NOT NULL,
        CreatedBy       INT NOT NULL,
        CustomerName    NVARCHAR(120) NOT NULL,
        CustomerPhone   NVARCHAR(20) NULL,
        GuestCount      INT NOT NULL,
        ReservationAt   DATETIME2(0) NOT NULL,
        ReservationStatus NVARCHAR(20) NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_TableReservations_Status DEFAULT (N'Booked'),
        Note            NVARCHAR(300) NULL,
        CONSTRAINT FK_TableReservations_Tables FOREIGN KEY (TableId) REFERENCES dbo.CafeTables(TableId),
        CONSTRAINT FK_TableReservations_Employees FOREIGN KEY (CreatedBy) REFERENCES dbo.Employees(EmployeeId),
        CONSTRAINT CK_TableReservations_GuestCount CHECK (GuestCount > 0),
        CONSTRAINT CK_TableReservations_Status CHECK (ReservationStatus IN (N'Booked', N'Arrived', N'Cancelled', N'Completed'))
    );
END;
GO

/* 5. Orders, order items, vouchers and payments */
IF OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.Orders', N'U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.Orders
    (
        OrderId          INT IDENTITY(1,1) CONSTRAINT PK_Orders PRIMARY KEY,
        OrderCode        NVARCHAR(30) NOT NULL CONSTRAINT UQ_Orders_OrderCode UNIQUE,
        TableId          INT NULL,
        CreatedBy        INT NOT NULL,
        OrderType        NVARCHAR(20) NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_Orders_OrderType DEFAULT (N'DineIn'),
        OrderStatus      NVARCHAR(25) NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_Orders_OrderStatus DEFAULT (N'New'),
        Subtotal         DECIMAL(14,2) NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_Orders_Subtotal DEFAULT (0),
        DiscountAmount   DECIMAL(14,2) NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_Orders_DiscountAmount DEFAULT (0),
        TotalAmount      DECIMAL(14,2) NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_Orders_TotalAmount DEFAULT (0),
        Note             NVARCHAR(500) NULL,
        CreatedAt        DATETIME2(0) NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_Orders_CreatedAt DEFAULT (SYSDATETIME()),
        CompletedAt      DATETIME2(0) NULL,
        CONSTRAINT FK_Orders_CafeTables FOREIGN KEY (TableId) REFERENCES dbo.CafeTables(TableId),
        CONSTRAINT FK_Orders_Employees FOREIGN KEY (CreatedBy) REFERENCES dbo.Employees(EmployeeId),
        CONSTRAINT CK_Orders_OrderType CHECK (OrderType IN (N'DineIn', N'Takeaway')),
        CONSTRAINT CK_Orders_OrderStatus CHECK (OrderStatus IN (N'New', N'Preparing', N'Ready', N'Completed', N'Cancelled')),
        CONSTRAINT CK_Orders_Amounts CHECK (Subtotal >= 0 AND DiscountAmount >= 0 AND TotalAmount >= 0)
    );
END;
GO

IF NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM sys.indexes
    WHERE name = N'IX_Orders_CreatedAt_Status'
      AND object_id = OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.Orders')
)
BEGIN
    CREATE INDEX IX_Orders_CreatedAt_Status ON dbo.Orders(CreatedAt, OrderStatus);
END;
GO

IF OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.OrderItems', N'U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.OrderItems
    (
        OrderItemId       INT IDENTITY(1,1) CONSTRAINT PK_OrderItems PRIMARY KEY,
        OrderId           INT NOT NULL,
        ProductSizeId     INT NOT NULL,
        ProductNameSnapshot NVARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,
        SizeNameSnapshot  NVARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,
        UnitPrice         DECIMAL(12,2) NOT NULL,
        Quantity          INT NOT NULL,
        LineTotal         DECIMAL(14,2) NOT NULL,
        PreparationStatus NVARCHAR(20) NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_OrderItems_PreparationStatus DEFAULT (N'Queued'),
        Note              NVARCHAR(300) NULL,
        CONSTRAINT FK_OrderItems_Orders FOREIGN KEY (OrderId) REFERENCES dbo.Orders(OrderId),
        CONSTRAINT FK_OrderItems_ProductSizes FOREIGN KEY (ProductSizeId) REFERENCES dbo.ProductSizes(ProductSizeId),
        CONSTRAINT CK_OrderItems_UnitPrice CHECK (UnitPrice >= 0),
        CONSTRAINT CK_OrderItems_Quantity CHECK (Quantity > 0),
        CONSTRAINT CK_OrderItems_LineTotal CHECK (LineTotal >= 0),
        CONSTRAINT CK_OrderItems_PreparationStatus CHECK (PreparationStatus IN (N'Queued', N'Preparing', N'Ready', N'Served', N'Cancelled'))
    );
END;
GO

IF NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM sys.indexes
    WHERE name = N'IX_OrderItems_OrderId'
      AND object_id = OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.OrderItems')
)
BEGIN
    CREATE INDEX IX_OrderItems_OrderId ON dbo.OrderItems(OrderId);
END;
GO

IF OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.OrderItemToppings', N'U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.OrderItemToppings
    (
        OrderItemToppingId  INT IDENTITY(1,1) CONSTRAINT PK_OrderItemToppings PRIMARY KEY,
        OrderItemId         INT NOT NULL,
        ToppingId           INT NOT NULL,
        ToppingNameSnapshot NVARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
        ExtraPrice          DECIMAL(12,2) NOT NULL,
        Quantity            INT NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_OrderItemToppings_Quantity DEFAULT (1),
        CONSTRAINT FK_OrderItemToppings_OrderItems FOREIGN KEY (OrderItemId) REFERENCES dbo.OrderItems(OrderItemId),
        CONSTRAINT FK_OrderItemToppings_Toppings FOREIGN KEY (ToppingId) REFERENCES dbo.Toppings(ToppingId),
        CONSTRAINT CK_OrderItemToppings_ExtraPrice CHECK (ExtraPrice >= 0),
        CONSTRAINT CK_OrderItemToppings_Quantity CHECK (Quantity > 0)
    );
END;
GO

IF OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.Vouchers', N'U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.Vouchers
    (
        VoucherId       INT IDENTITY(1,1) CONSTRAINT PK_Vouchers PRIMARY KEY,
        VoucherCode     NVARCHAR(40) NOT NULL CONSTRAINT UQ_Vouchers_VoucherCode UNIQUE,
        Description     NVARCHAR(250) NULL,
        DiscountType    NVARCHAR(10) NOT NULL,
        DiscountValue   DECIMAL(12,2) NOT NULL,
        MinimumOrder    DECIMAL(12,2) NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_Vouchers_MinimumOrder DEFAULT (0),
        MaximumDiscount DECIMAL(12,2) NULL,
        StartAt         DATETIME2(0) NOT NULL,
        EndAt           DATETIME2(0) NOT NULL,
        UsageLimit      INT NULL,
        IsActive        BIT NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_Vouchers_IsActive DEFAULT (1),
        CONSTRAINT CK_Vouchers_DiscountType CHECK (DiscountType IN (N'Percent', N'Amount')),
        CONSTRAINT CK_Vouchers_DiscountValue CHECK (DiscountValue > 0),
        CONSTRAINT CK_Vouchers_MinimumOrder CHECK (MinimumOrder >= 0),
        CONSTRAINT CK_Vouchers_MaximumDiscount CHECK (MaximumDiscount IS NULL OR MaximumDiscount >= 0),
        CONSTRAINT CK_Vouchers_Dates CHECK (EndAt > StartAt),
        CONSTRAINT CK_Vouchers_UsageLimit CHECK (UsageLimit IS NULL OR UsageLimit > 0),
        CONSTRAINT CK_Vouchers_Percent CHECK (DiscountType <> N'Percent' OR DiscountValue <= 100)
    );
END;
GO

IF OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.OrderVouchers', N'U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.OrderVouchers
    (
        OrderId          INT NOT NULL,
        VoucherId        INT NOT NULL,
        DiscountApplied  DECIMAL(12,2) NOT NULL,
        CONSTRAINT PK_OrderVouchers PRIMARY KEY (OrderId, VoucherId),
        CONSTRAINT FK_OrderVouchers_Orders FOREIGN KEY (OrderId) REFERENCES dbo.Orders(OrderId),
        CONSTRAINT FK_OrderVouchers_Vouchers FOREIGN KEY (VoucherId) REFERENCES dbo.Vouchers(VoucherId),
        CONSTRAINT CK_OrderVouchers_DiscountApplied CHECK (DiscountApplied >= 0)
    );
END;
GO

IF OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.Payments', N'U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.Payments
    (
        PaymentId       INT IDENTITY(1,1) CONSTRAINT PK_Payments PRIMARY KEY,
        OrderId         INT NOT NULL,
        ProcessedBy     INT NOT NULL,
        PaymentMethod   NVARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
        PaymentStatus   NVARCHAR(20) NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_Payments_Status DEFAULT (N'Pending'),
        Amount          DECIMAL(14,2) NOT NULL,
        TransactionRef  NVARCHAR(120) NULL,
        PaidAt          DATETIME2(0) NULL,
        CreatedAt       DATETIME2(0) NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_Payments_CreatedAt DEFAULT (SYSDATETIME()),
        Note            NVARCHAR(300) NULL,
        CONSTRAINT FK_Payments_Orders FOREIGN KEY (OrderId) REFERENCES dbo.Orders(OrderId),
        CONSTRAINT FK_Payments_Employees FOREIGN KEY (ProcessedBy) REFERENCES dbo.Employees(EmployeeId),
        CONSTRAINT CK_Payments_Method CHECK (PaymentMethod IN (N'Cash', N'BankQR')),
        CONSTRAINT CK_Payments_Status CHECK (PaymentStatus IN (N'Pending', N'Success', N'Failed', N'Refunded')),
        CONSTRAINT CK_Payments_Amount CHECK (Amount >= 0)
    );
END;
GO

IF NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM sys.indexes
    WHERE name = N'IX_Payments_OrderId_Status'
      AND object_id = OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.Payments')
)
BEGIN
    CREATE INDEX IX_Payments_OrderId_Status ON dbo.Payments(OrderId, PaymentStatus);
END;
GO

IF OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.OrderStatusHistory', N'U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.OrderStatusHistory
    (
        OrderStatusHistoryId BIGINT IDENTITY(1,1) CONSTRAINT PK_OrderStatusHistory PRIMARY KEY,
        OrderId              INT NOT NULL,
        ChangedBy            INT NULL,
        OldStatus            NVARCHAR(25) NULL,
        NewStatus            NVARCHAR(25) NOT NULL,
        ChangedAt            DATETIME2(0) NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_OrderStatusHistory_ChangedAt DEFAULT (SYSDATETIME()),
        Note                 NVARCHAR(300) NULL,
        CONSTRAINT FK_OrderStatusHistory_Orders FOREIGN KEY (OrderId) REFERENCES dbo.Orders(OrderId),
        CONSTRAINT FK_OrderStatusHistory_Employees FOREIGN KEY (ChangedBy) REFERENCES dbo.Employees(EmployeeId)
    );
END;
GO

/* 6. Audit log */
IF OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.AuditLogs', N'U') IS NULL
BEGIN
    CREATE TABLE dbo.AuditLogs
    (
        AuditLogId    BIGINT IDENTITY(1,1) CONSTRAINT PK_AuditLogs PRIMARY KEY,
        EmployeeId    INT NULL,
        Action        NVARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
        EntityName    NVARCHAR(100) NULL,
        EntityId      NVARCHAR(50) NULL,
        Details       NVARCHAR(1000) NULL,
        CreatedAt     DATETIME2(0) NOT NULL CONSTRAINT DF_AuditLogs_CreatedAt DEFAULT (SYSDATETIME()),
        CONSTRAINT FK_AuditLogs_Employees FOREIGN KEY (EmployeeId) REFERENCES dbo.Employees(EmployeeId)
    );
END;
GO

IF NOT EXISTS (
    SELECT 1 FROM sys.indexes
    WHERE name = N'IX_AuditLogs_CreatedAt'
      AND object_id = OBJECT_ID(N'dbo.AuditLogs')
)
BEGIN
    CREATE INDEX IX_AuditLogs_CreatedAt ON dbo.AuditLogs(CreatedAt);
END;
GO

/* 7. Reset all existing data before seeding.
   Delete child rows before parent rows to respect foreign keys. */
SET XACT_ABORT ON;
BEGIN TRY
    BEGIN TRANSACTION;

    DELETE FROM dbo.AuditLogs;
    DELETE FROM dbo.InventoryTransactions;
    DELETE FROM dbo.OrderStatusHistory;
    DELETE FROM dbo.OrderVouchers;
    DELETE FROM dbo.Payments;
    DELETE FROM dbo.OrderItemToppings;
    DELETE FROM dbo.OrderItems;
    DELETE FROM dbo.Orders;
    DELETE FROM dbo.TableReservations;
    DELETE FROM dbo.StockReceiptDetails;
    DELETE FROM dbo.StockReceipts;
    DELETE FROM dbo.ProductRecipes;
    DELETE FROM dbo.ToppingRecipes;
    DELETE FROM dbo.ProductSizes;
    DELETE FROM dbo.Products;
    DELETE FROM dbo.Categories;
    DELETE FROM dbo.Toppings;
    DELETE FROM dbo.Ingredients;
    DELETE FROM dbo.Suppliers;
    DELETE FROM dbo.Vouchers;
    DELETE FROM dbo.CafeTables;
    DELETE FROM dbo.Employees;
    DELETE FROM dbo.Roles;

    COMMIT TRANSACTION;
    PRINT N'Existing data cleared successfully.';
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK TRANSACTION;
    THROW;
END CATCH;
GO

/* 8. Reset identity counters after data deletion.
   For an empty table, RESEED, 0 makes the next generated ID 1. */
DBCC CHECKIDENT (N'dbo.Roles', RESEED, 0);
DBCC CHECKIDENT (N'dbo.Employees', RESEED, 0);
DBCC CHECKIDENT (N'dbo.Categories', RESEED, 0);
DBCC CHECKIDENT (N'dbo.Products', RESEED, 0);
DBCC CHECKIDENT (N'dbo.ProductSizes', RESEED, 0);
DBCC CHECKIDENT (N'dbo.Toppings', RESEED, 0);
DBCC CHECKIDENT (N'dbo.Ingredients', RESEED, 0);
DBCC CHECKIDENT (N'dbo.Suppliers', RESEED, 0);
DBCC CHECKIDENT (N'dbo.StockReceipts', RESEED, 0);
DBCC CHECKIDENT (N'dbo.StockReceiptDetails', RESEED, 0);
DBCC CHECKIDENT (N'dbo.InventoryTransactions', RESEED, 0);
DBCC CHECKIDENT (N'dbo.CafeTables', RESEED, 0);
DBCC CHECKIDENT (N'dbo.TableReservations', RESEED, 0);
DBCC CHECKIDENT (N'dbo.Orders', RESEED, 0);
DBCC CHECKIDENT (N'dbo.OrderItems', RESEED, 0);
DBCC CHECKIDENT (N'dbo.OrderItemToppings', RESEED, 0);
DBCC CHECKIDENT (N'dbo.Vouchers', RESEED, 0);
DBCC CHECKIDENT (N'dbo.Payments', RESEED, 0);
DBCC CHECKIDENT (N'dbo.AuditLogs', RESEED, 0);
DBCC CHECKIDENT (N'dbo.OrderStatusHistory', RESEED, 0);
GO

/* 9. Seed the four default roles. */
INSERT INTO dbo.Roles (RoleName, Description)
VALUES
    (N'Admin', N'Full system administration'),
    (N'Cashier', N'Orders, checkout and payments'),
    (N'Barista', N'View and update drink preparation status'),
    (N'Warehouse Manager', N'Ingredients, suppliers and stock receipts');
GO

/* 10. Seed default cafe tables. */
INSERT INTO dbo.CafeTables (TableName, Capacity, TableStatus)
VALUES
    (N'T01', 2, N'Available'),
    (N'T02', 2, N'Available'),
    (N'T03', 4, N'Available'),
    (N'T04', 4, N'Available'),
    (N'T05', 6, N'Available');
GO

/* 11. Seed initial Admin account.
   This hash must match the password-hashing/verification implementation in the app.
   Keep only one canonical hash here; do not overwrite it later in the script. */
INSERT INTO dbo.Employees
(
    RoleId,
    Username,
    PasswordHash,
    FullName,
    IsActive,
    CreatedAt
)
SELECT
    RoleId,
    N'admin',
    N'wl33f8L0tpPQP/C5In8k3g==:Br1TEN/0d4b+urSt+/hQ1MnwoI2bRVBuEc36rIDcRwE=',
    N'System Administrator',
    1,
    SYSDATETIME()
FROM dbo.Roles
WHERE RoleName = N'Admin';
GO

/* 12. Seed test accounts for role-based access testing.
   Test password for all seeded accounts: 123456
   Hash format: Base64(salt):Base64(PBKDF2-SHA256 hash)
   PBKDF2-SHA256: 100,000 iterations, 16-byte salt, 32-byte derived key.
   These hashes were generated for the PasswordService implementation used by CoffeeRuu.
*/

INSERT INTO dbo.Employees
(
    RoleId,
    Username,
    PasswordHash,
    FullName,
    Phone,
    Email,
    IsActive,
    CreatedAt
)
SELECT
    r.RoleId,
    v.Username,
    v.PasswordHash,
    v.FullName,
    v.Phone,
    v.Email,
    1,
    SYSDATETIME()
FROM
(
    VALUES
        (N'cashier', N'7ZBThz4Ju941ul+IqzDLFA==:fxWE0TfqCpud5krU8uyrneYln8KscJrZhAzrZWsU5Rc=', N'Test Cashier', N'0900000001', N'cashier@coffeeruu.local', N'Cashier'),
        (N'barista', N'7TNiXVhDyua+u2K6TrMEbQ==:NBKZyyG6ARCczjkSBT2fYmy8kxq7IqqZQfoufuQ/4Sc=', N'Test Barista', N'0900000002', N'barista@coffeeruu.local', N'Barista'),
        (N'warehouse', N'lmE5yWUtLJwML1p83eUXgw==:+Q1qN1ikm7kmC/Md7q/uOKHjqW3btCBllaxie3edjaQ=', N'Test Warehouse Manager', N'0900000003', N'warehouse@coffeeruu.local', N'Warehouse Manager')
) AS v(Username, PasswordHash, FullName, Phone, Email, RoleName)
INNER JOIN dbo.Roles AS r ON r.RoleName = v.RoleName;
GO

/* 13. Verify seeded test accounts without exposing password hashes. */
SELECT
    e.EmployeeId,
    e.Username,
    e.FullName,
    r.RoleName,
    e.IsActive
FROM dbo.Employees AS e
INNER JOIN dbo.Roles AS r ON r.RoleId = e.RoleId
ORDER BY e.EmployeeId;
GO

/* 13.3. Insert products */
INSERT INTO dbo.Products
    (CategoryId, ProductName, Description, IsAvailable)
SELECT
    C.CategoryId,
    N.ProductName,
    N.Description,
    1
FROM @NewProducts AS N
INNER JOIN dbo.Categories AS C
    ON C.CategoryName = N.CategoryName;

/* 13.4. Insert product sizes and prices */
INSERT INTO dbo.ProductSizes
    (ProductId, SizeName, Price, IsAvailable)
SELECT
    P.ProductId,
    S.SizeName,
    S.Price,
    1
FROM @NewProducts AS N
INNER JOIN dbo.Products AS P
    ON P.ProductName = N.ProductName
CROSS APPLY
(
    VALUES
        (N'Nhỏ (S)', N.PriceS),
        (N'Lớn (M)', N.PriceM),
        (N'Rất lớn (L)', N.PriceL)
) AS S(SizeName, Price)
WHERE S.Price IS NOT NULL;
GO

    ) AS r(ToppingName, IngredientName, QuantityUsed)
    INNER JOIN dbo.Toppings t
        ON t.ToppingName = r.ToppingName
    INNER JOIN dbo.Ingredients i
        ON i.IngredientName = r.IngredientName;


    /* =========================================================
       6. STOCK RECEIPTS AND DETAILS
       ========================================================= */

    DECLARE @WarehouseId INT =
    (
        SELECT TOP (1) EmployeeId
        FROM dbo.Employees
        WHERE Username = N'warehouse'
    );

    IF @WarehouseId IS NULL
        SELECT @WarehouseId = EmployeeId
        FROM dbo.Employees e
        INNER JOIN dbo.Roles r ON r.RoleId = e.RoleId
        WHERE r.RoleName = N'Warehouse Manager';

    IF @WarehouseId IS NULL
        SELECT @WarehouseId = EmployeeId
        FROM dbo.Employees
        WHERE Username = N'admin';

    INSERT INTO dbo.StockReceipts
        (SupplierId, ReceivedBy, ReceiptDate, Note, TotalCost)
    SELECT
        s.SupplierId, @WarehouseId, DATEADD(DAY, -7, SYSDATETIME()),
        N'Phiếu nhập nguyên liệu demo - đợt 1', 0
    FROM dbo.Suppliers s
    WHERE s.SupplierName = N'Công ty Cà phê Tây Nguyên';

    DECLARE @Receipt1 INT = CONVERT(INT, SCOPE_IDENTITY());

    INSERT INTO dbo.StockReceiptDetails
        (StockReceiptId, IngredientId, Quantity, UnitCost)
    SELECT
        @Receipt1, i.IngredientId, v.Quantity, v.UnitCost
    FROM
    (
        VALUES
        (N'Hạt cà phê', 10.000, 180000.00),
        (N'Trà đen', 4.000, 120000.00),
        (N'Trà ô long', 3.000, 150000.00)
    ) AS v(IngredientName, Quantity, UnitCost)
    INNER JOIN dbo.Ingredients i
        ON i.IngredientName = v.IngredientName;

    UPDATE dbo.StockReceipts
    SET TotalCost =
    (
        SELECT SUM(Quantity * UnitCost)
        FROM dbo.StockReceiptDetails
        WHERE StockReceiptId = @Receipt1
    )
    WHERE StockReceiptId = @Receipt1;


    INSERT INTO dbo.StockReceipts
        (SupplierId, ReceivedBy, ReceiptDate, Note, TotalCost)
    SELECT
        s.SupplierId, @WarehouseId, DATEADD(DAY, -3, SYSDATETIME()),
        N'Phiếu nhập sữa và nguyên liệu pha chế - đợt 2', 0
    FROM dbo.Suppliers s
    WHERE s.SupplierName = N'Nhà phân phối Nguyên liệu Pha chế';

    DECLARE @Receipt2 INT = CONVERT(INT, SCOPE_IDENTITY());

    INSERT INTO dbo.StockReceiptDetails
        (StockReceiptId, IngredientId, Quantity, UnitCost)
    SELECT
        @Receipt2, i.IngredientId, v.Quantity, v.UnitCost
    FROM
    (
        VALUES
        (N'Sữa tươi', 20.000, 32000.00),
        (N'Sữa đặc', 8.000, 65000.00),
        (N'Trân châu khô', 5.000, 45000.00),
        (N'Bột matcha', 2.000, 280000.00),
        (N'Bột chocolate', 2.000, 160000.00)
    ) AS v(IngredientName, Quantity, UnitCost)
    INNER JOIN dbo.Ingredients i
        ON i.IngredientName = v.IngredientName;

    UPDATE dbo.StockReceipts
    SET TotalCost =
    (
        SELECT SUM(Quantity * UnitCost)
        FROM dbo.StockReceiptDetails
        WHERE StockReceiptId = @Receipt2
    )
    WHERE StockReceiptId = @Receipt2;


    INSERT INTO dbo.StockReceipts
        (SupplierId, ReceivedBy, ReceiptDate, Note, TotalCost)
    SELECT
        s.SupplierId, @WarehouseId, DATEADD(DAY, -1, SYSDATETIME()),
        N'Phiếu nhập nông sản và đồ ăn vặt - đợt 3', 0
    FROM dbo.Suppliers s
    WHERE s.SupplierName = N'Nông sản Miền Tây';

    DECLARE @Receipt3 INT = CONVERT(INT, SCOPE_IDENTITY());

    INSERT INTO dbo.StockReceiptDetails
        (StockReceiptId, IngredientId, Quantity, UnitCost)
    SELECT
        @Receipt3, i.IngredientId, v.Quantity, v.UnitCost
    FROM
    (
        VALUES
        (N'Cam tươi', 5.000, 30000.00),
        (N'Chanh tươi', 4.000, 25000.00),
        (N'Khoai tây', 10.000, 22000.00),
        (N'Xúc xích', 30.000, 9000.00)
    ) AS v(IngredientName, Quantity, UnitCost)
    INNER JOIN dbo.Ingredients i
        ON i.IngredientName = v.IngredientName;

    UPDATE dbo.StockReceipts
    SET TotalCost =
    (
        SELECT SUM(Quantity * UnitCost)
        FROM dbo.StockReceiptDetails
        WHERE StockReceiptId = @Receipt3
    )
    WHERE StockReceiptId = @Receipt3;


    /* =========================================================
       7. INVENTORY TRANSACTIONS
       These records are sample history, not stock adjustments.
       ========================================================= */

    INSERT INTO dbo.InventoryTransactions
        (IngredientId, EmployeeId, TransactionType,
         QuantityChange, ReferenceType, ReferenceId, CreatedAt, Note)
    SELECT
        i.IngredientId, @WarehouseId, N'StockIn',
        d.Quantity, N'StockReceipt', d.StockReceiptId,
        sr.ReceiptDate, sr.Note
    FROM dbo.StockReceiptDetails d
    INNER JOIN dbo.StockReceipts sr
        ON sr.StockReceiptId = d.StockReceiptId
    INNER JOIN dbo.Ingredients i
        ON i.IngredientId = d.IngredientId
    WHERE d.StockReceiptId IN (@Receipt1, @Receipt2, @Receipt3);

    INSERT INTO dbo.InventoryTransactions
        (IngredientId, EmployeeId, TransactionType,
         QuantityChange, ReferenceType, ReferenceId, CreatedAt, Note)
    SELECT
        i.IngredientId, @WarehouseId, N'Adjustment',
        -2.000, N'DemoAdjustment', NULL,
        DATEADD(HOUR, -5, SYSDATETIME()),
        N'Ví dụ điều chỉnh tồn kho khi kiểm kê'
    FROM dbo.Ingredients i
    WHERE i.IngredientName = N'Sữa tươi';


    /* =========================================================
       8. TABLE RESERVATIONS
       ========================================================= */

    DECLARE @CashierId INT =
    (
        SELECT TOP (1) EmployeeId
        FROM dbo.Employees
        WHERE Username = N'cashier'
    );

    IF @CashierId IS NULL
        SELECT @CashierId = EmployeeId
        FROM dbo.Employees
        WHERE Username = N'admin';

    INSERT INTO dbo.TableReservations
        (TableId, CreatedBy, CustomerName, CustomerPhone,
         GuestCount, ReservationAt, ReservationStatus, Note)
    SELECT
        t.TableId, @CashierId, v.CustomerName, v.CustomerPhone,
        v.GuestCount, v.ReservationAt, v.ReservationStatus, v.Note
    FROM
    (
        VALUES
        (N'T01', N'Nguyễn Minh Anh', N'0912000001', 2,
         DATEADD(HOUR, 2, SYSDATETIME()), N'Booked', N'Khách đặt bàn trước'),

        (N'T02', N'Trần Hoàng Nam', N'0912000002', 2,
         DATEADD(HOUR, 4, SYSDATETIME()), N'Booked', N'Khách hẹn gặp bạn'),

        (N'T03', N'Lê Thảo Vy', N'0912000003', 4,
         DATEADD(DAY, -1, SYSDATETIME()), N'Completed', N'Đã phục vụ xong'),

        (N'T04', N'Phạm Quốc Bảo', N'0912000004', 3,
         DATEADD(DAY, 1, SYSDATETIME()), N'Cancelled', N'Khách đã hủy đặt bàn')
    ) AS v(TableName, CustomerName, CustomerPhone,
           GuestCount, ReservationAt, ReservationStatus, Note)
    INNER JOIN dbo.CafeTables t
        ON t.TableName = v.TableName;

    /* =========================================================
       9. VOUCHERS
       ========================================================= */

    INSERT INTO dbo.Vouchers
        (VoucherCode, Description, DiscountType, DiscountValue,
         MinimumOrder, MaximumDiscount, StartAt, EndAt,
         UsageLimit, IsActive)
    VALUES
        (N'WELCOME10', N'Giảm 10% cho đơn hàng đủ điều kiện',
         N'Percent', 10, 50000, 20000,
         DATEADD(DAY, -30, SYSDATETIME()),
         DATEADD(DAY, 30, SYSDATETIME()), 100, 1),

        (N'COFFEE15000', N'Giảm 15.000 đồng cho đơn hàng từ 100.000 đồng',
         N'Amount', 15000, 100000, NULL,
         DATEADD(DAY, -10, SYSDATETIME()),
         DATEADD(DAY, 20, SYSDATETIME()), 50, 1),

        (N'EXPIRED5', N'Voucher demo đã hết hạn',
         N'Percent', 5, 0, 10000,
         DATEADD(DAY, -60, SYSDATETIME()),
         DATEADD(DAY, -30, SYSDATETIME()), 100, 1),

        (N'INACTIVE20', N'Voucher demo đã ngừng sử dụng',
         N'Percent', 20, 50000, 30000,
         DATEADD(DAY, -5, SYSDATETIME()),
         DATEADD(DAY, 30, SYSDATETIME()), 20, 0);


    /* =========================================================
       10. DEMO ORDERS
       ========================================================= */

    INSERT INTO dbo.Orders
        (OrderCode, TableId, CreatedBy, OrderType, OrderStatus,
         Subtotal, DiscountAmount, TotalAmount, Note,
         CreatedAt, CompletedAt)
    SELECT
        v.OrderCode, t.TableId, e.EmployeeId, v.OrderType,
        v.OrderStatus, v.Subtotal, v.DiscountAmount, v.TotalAmount,
        v.Note, v.CreatedAt, v.CompletedAt
    FROM
    (
        VALUES
        (N'CR-DEMO-001', N'T01', N'cashier', N'DineIn', N'Completed',
         68000.00, 6800.00, 61200.00, N'Đơn demo đã thanh toán',
         DATEADD(HOUR, -5, SYSDATETIME()),
         DATEADD(HOUR, -4, SYSDATETIME())),

        (N'CR-DEMO-002', N'T02', N'cashier', N'DineIn', N'Preparing',
         81000.00, 0.00, 81000.00, N'Đơn demo đang pha chế',
         DATEADD(MINUTE, -20, SYSDATETIME()), NULL),

        (N'CR-DEMO-003', NULL, N'cashier', N'Takeaway', N'New',
         50000.00, 0.00, 50000.00, N'Đơn mang đi mới tạo',
         DATEADD(MINUTE, -5, SYSDATETIME()), NULL),

        (N'CR-DEMO-004', NULL, N'cashier', N'Takeaway', N'Cancelled',
         30000.00, 0.00, 30000.00, N'Đơn demo đã hủy',
         DATEADD(DAY, -1, SYSDATETIME()), NULL)
    ) AS v(OrderCode, TableName, Username, OrderType, OrderStatus,
           Subtotal, DiscountAmount, TotalAmount, Note, CreatedAt, CompletedAt)
    INNER JOIN dbo.Employees e ON e.Username = v.Username
    LEFT JOIN dbo.CafeTables t ON t.TableName = v.TableName;


    /* =========================================================
       11. ORDER ITEMS
       ========================================================= */

    INSERT INTO dbo.OrderItems
        (OrderId, ProductSizeId, ProductNameSnapshot,
         SizeNameSnapshot, UnitPrice, Quantity, LineTotal,
         PreparationStatus, Note)
    SELECT
        o.OrderId, ps.ProductSizeId, p.ProductName,
        ps.SizeName, ps.Price, v.Quantity,
        ps.Price * v.Quantity, v.PreparationStatus, v.Note
    FROM
    (
        VALUES
        (N'CR-DEMO-001', N'Bạc xỉu', N'Nhỏ (S)', 1, N'Served', N'Ít đá'),
        (N'CR-DEMO-001', N'Trà đào cam sả', N'Nhỏ (S)', 1, N'Served', N''),
        (N'CR-DEMO-001', N'Bánh croissant', N'Nhỏ (S)', 1, N'Served', N''),

        (N'CR-DEMO-002', N'Latte', N'Lớn (M)', 1, N'Preparing', N''),
        (N'CR-DEMO-002', N'Trà sữa truyền thống', N'Lớn (M)', 1, N'Queued', N'50% đường'),

        (N'CR-DEMO-003', N'Cà phê đen đá', N'Nhỏ (S)', 1, N'Queued', N'Ít đá'),
        (N'CR-DEMO-003', N'Bánh mì que', N'Nhỏ (S)', 1, N'Queued', N'')
    ) AS v(OrderCode, ProductName, SizeName, Quantity, PreparationStatus, Note)
    INNER JOIN dbo.Orders o ON o.OrderCode = v.OrderCode
    INNER JOIN dbo.Products p ON p.ProductName = v.ProductName
    INNER JOIN dbo.ProductSizes ps
        ON ps.ProductId = p.ProductId AND ps.SizeName = v.SizeName;


    /* =========================================================
       12. ORDER ITEM TOPPINGS
       ========================================================= */

    INSERT INTO dbo.OrderItemToppings
        (OrderItemId, ToppingId, ToppingNameSnapshot, ExtraPrice, Quantity)
    SELECT
        oi.OrderItemId, t.ToppingId, t.ToppingName, t.ExtraPrice, 1
    FROM dbo.OrderItems oi
    INNER JOIN dbo.Orders o ON o.OrderId = oi.OrderId
    INNER JOIN dbo.Toppings t
        ON t.ToppingName = N'Trân châu đen'
    WHERE o.OrderCode = N'CR-DEMO-002'
      AND oi.ProductNameSnapshot = N'Trà sữa truyền thống';

    INSERT INTO dbo.OrderItemToppings
        (OrderItemId, ToppingId, ToppingNameSnapshot, ExtraPrice, Quantity)
    SELECT
        oi.OrderItemId, t.ToppingId, t.ToppingName, t.ExtraPrice, 1
    FROM dbo.OrderItems oi
    INNER JOIN dbo.Orders o ON o.OrderId = oi.OrderId
    INNER JOIN dbo.Toppings t
        ON t.ToppingName = N'Kem cheese'
    WHERE o.OrderCode = N'CR-DEMO-002'
      AND oi.ProductNameSnapshot = N'Latte';


    /* =========================================================
       13. ORDER VOUCHERS
       ========================================================= */

    INSERT INTO dbo.OrderVouchers (OrderId, VoucherId, DiscountApplied)
    SELECT o.OrderId, v.VoucherId, 6800.00
    FROM dbo.Orders o
    CROSS JOIN dbo.Vouchers v
    WHERE o.OrderCode = N'CR-DEMO-001'
      AND v.VoucherCode = N'WELCOME10';


    /* =========================================================
       14. PAYMENTS
       ========================================================= */

    INSERT INTO dbo.Payments
        (OrderId, ProcessedBy, PaymentMethod, PaymentStatus,
         Amount, TransactionRef, PaidAt, CreatedAt, Note)
    SELECT
        o.OrderId, e.EmployeeId, v.PaymentMethod, v.PaymentStatus,
        v.Amount, v.TransactionRef, v.PaidAt, v.CreatedAt, v.Note
    FROM
    (
        VALUES
        (N'CR-DEMO-001', N'Cashier', N'Cash', N'Success',
         61200.00, N'PAY-DEMO-001',
         DATEADD(HOUR, -4, SYSDATETIME()),
         DATEADD(HOUR, -4, SYSDATETIME()), N'Thanh toán tiền mặt'),

        (N'CR-DEMO-002', N'Cashier', N'BankQR', N'Pending',
         81000.00, N'PAY-DEMO-002', NULL,
         DATEADD(MINUTE, -10, SYSDATETIME()), N'Chờ xác nhận thanh toán QR'),

        (N'CR-DEMO-004', N'Cashier', N'Cash', N'Failed',
         30000.00, N'PAY-DEMO-004', NULL,
         DATEADD(DAY, -1, SYSDATETIME()), N'Thanh toán thất bại')
    ) AS v(OrderCode, RoleName, PaymentMethod, PaymentStatus,
           Amount, TransactionRef, PaidAt, CreatedAt, Note)
    INNER JOIN dbo.Orders o ON o.OrderCode = v.OrderCode
    INNER JOIN dbo.Roles r ON r.RoleName = v.RoleName
    INNER JOIN dbo.Employees e ON e.RoleId = r.RoleId
        AND e.Username = N'cashier';


    /* =========================================================
       15. ORDER STATUS HISTORY
       ========================================================= */

    INSERT INTO dbo.OrderStatusHistory
        (OrderId, ChangedBy, OldStatus, NewStatus, ChangedAt, Note)
    SELECT
        o.OrderId, e.EmployeeId, h.OldStatus, h.NewStatus,
        h.ChangedAt, h.Note
    FROM
    (
        VALUES
        (N'CR-DEMO-001', N'New', N'Preparing',
         DATEADD(HOUR, -5, SYSDATETIME()), N'Bắt đầu pha chế'),

        (N'CR-DEMO-001', N'Preparing', N'Ready',
         DATEADD(HOUR, -4, SYSDATETIME()), N'Hoàn thành pha chế'),

        (N'CR-DEMO-001', N'Ready', N'Completed',
         DATEADD(HOUR, -4, SYSDATETIME()), N'Đã giao món cho khách'),

        (N'CR-DEMO-002', N'New', N'Preparing',
         DATEADD(MINUTE, -15, SYSDATETIME()), N'Barista nhận đơn')
    ) AS h(OrderCode, OldStatus, NewStatus, ChangedAt, Note)
    INNER JOIN dbo.Orders o ON o.OrderCode = h.OrderCode
    LEFT JOIN dbo.Employees e ON e.Username = N'barista';


    /* =========================================================
       16. AUDIT LOGS
       ========================================================= */

    INSERT INTO dbo.AuditLogs
        (EmployeeId, Action, EntityName, EntityId, Details, CreatedAt)
    SELECT
        e.EmployeeId, v.Action, v.EntityName, v.EntityId,
        v.Details, v.CreatedAt
    FROM
    (
        VALUES
        (N'admin', N'Login', N'Employees', N'1',
         N'Admin đăng nhập hệ thống thành công',
         DATEADD(HOUR, -8, SYSDATETIME())),

        (N'admin', N'Create', N'Products', NULL,
         N'Tạo dữ liệu menu demo',
         DATEADD(HOUR, -7, SYSDATETIME())),

        (N'warehouse', N'Create', N'StockReceipts', NULL,
         N'Tạo các phiếu nhập hàng demo',
         DATEADD(HOUR, -3, SYSDATETIME())),

        (N'cashier', N'Create', N'Orders', N'CR-DEMO-001',
         N'Tạo đơn hàng tại bàn T01',
         DATEADD(HOUR, -5, SYSDATETIME())),

        (N'cashier', N'Payment', N'Payments', N'PAY-DEMO-001',
         N'Thanh toán đơn CR-DEMO-001 thành công',
         DATEADD(HOUR, -4, SYSDATETIME())),

        (N'barista', N'UpdateStatus', N'OrderItems', NULL,
         N'Cập nhật trạng thái pha chế',
         DATEADD(MINUTE, -15, SYSDATETIME()))
    ) AS v(Username, Action, EntityName, EntityId, Details, CreatedAt)
    INNER JOIN dbo.Employees e ON e.Username = v.Username;

    COMMIT TRANSACTION;

    PRINT N'Demo seed data inserted successfully.';
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK TRANSACTION;

    THROW;
END CATCH;


/* =========================================================
   17. VERIFY DEMO DATA
   ========================================================= */

SELECT N'Roles' AS TableName, COUNT_BIG(*) AS TotalRows FROM dbo.Roles
UNION ALL SELECT N'Employees', COUNT_BIG(*) FROM dbo.Employees
UNION ALL SELECT N'Categories', COUNT_BIG(*) FROM dbo.Categories
UNION ALL SELECT N'Products', COUNT_BIG(*) FROM dbo.Products
UNION ALL SELECT N'ProductSizes', COUNT_BIG(*) FROM dbo.ProductSizes
UNION ALL SELECT N'Toppings', COUNT_BIG(*) FROM dbo.Toppings
UNION ALL SELECT N'Ingredients', COUNT_BIG(*) FROM dbo.Ingredients
UNION ALL SELECT N'ProductRecipes', COUNT_BIG(*) FROM dbo.ProductRecipes
UNION ALL SELECT N'ToppingRecipes', COUNT_BIG(*) FROM dbo.ToppingRecipes
UNION ALL SELECT N'Suppliers', COUNT_BIG(*) FROM dbo.Suppliers
UNION ALL SELECT N'StockReceipts', COUNT_BIG(*) FROM dbo.StockReceipts
UNION ALL SELECT N'StockReceiptDetails', COUNT_BIG(*) FROM dbo.StockReceiptDetails
UNION ALL SELECT N'InventoryTransactions', COUNT_BIG(*) FROM dbo.InventoryTransactions
UNION ALL SELECT N'CafeTables', COUNT_BIG(*) FROM dbo.CafeTables
UNION ALL SELECT N'TableReservations', COUNT_BIG(*) FROM dbo.TableReservations
UNION ALL SELECT N'Orders', COUNT_BIG(*) FROM dbo.Orders
UNION ALL SELECT N'OrderItems', COUNT_BIG(*) FROM dbo.OrderItems
UNION ALL SELECT N'OrderItemToppings', COUNT_BIG(*) FROM dbo.OrderItemToppings
UNION ALL SELECT N'Vouchers', COUNT_BIG(*) FROM dbo.Vouchers
UNION ALL SELECT N'OrderVouchers', COUNT_BIG(*) FROM dbo.OrderVouchers
UNION ALL SELECT N'Payments', COUNT_BIG(*) FROM dbo.Payments
UNION ALL SELECT N'OrderStatusHistory', COUNT_BIG(*) FROM dbo.OrderStatusHistory
UNION ALL SELECT N'AuditLogs', COUNT_BIG(*) FROM dbo.AuditLogs
ORDER BY TableName;
GO

trên đây là DB của tu, dung bang SQL server

phía dưới là phần code project dùng VS 2022 với .Net 8.
using System;
using System.Collections.Generic;
using CoffeeRuu.Models;
using Microsoft.EntityFrameworkCore;

namespace CoffeeRuu.Data;

public partial class CoffeeRuuDbContext : DbContext
{
    public CoffeeRuuDbContext(DbContextOptions<CoffeeRuuDbContext> options)
        : base(options)
    {
    }

    public virtual DbSet<AuditLog> AuditLogs { get; set; }

    public virtual DbSet<CafeTable> CafeTables { get; set; }

    public virtual DbSet<Category> Categories { get; set; }

    public virtual DbSet<Employee> Employees { get; set; }

    public virtual DbSet<Ingredient> Ingredients { get; set; }

    public virtual DbSet<InventoryTransaction> InventoryTransactions { get; set; }

    public virtual DbSet<Order> Orders { get; set; }

    public virtual DbSet<OrderItem> OrderItems { get; set; }

    public virtual DbSet<OrderItemTopping> OrderItemToppings { get; set; }

    public virtual DbSet<OrderStatusHistory> OrderStatusHistories { get; set; }

    public virtual DbSet<OrderVoucher> OrderVouchers { get; set; }

    public virtual DbSet<Payment> Payments { get; set; }

    public virtual DbSet<Product> Products { get; set; }

    public virtual DbSet<ProductRecipe> ProductRecipes { get; set; }

    public virtual DbSet<ProductSize> ProductSizes { get; set; }

    public virtual DbSet<Role> Roles { get; set; }

    public virtual DbSet<StockReceipt> StockReceipts { get; set; }

    public virtual DbSet<StockReceiptDetail> StockReceiptDetails { get; set; }

    public virtual DbSet<Supplier> Suppliers { get; set; }

    public virtual DbSet<TableReservation> TableReservations { get; set; }

    public virtual DbSet<Topping> Toppings { get; set; }

    public virtual DbSet<ToppingRecipe> ToppingRecipes { get; set; }

    public virtual DbSet<Voucher> Vouchers { get; set; }

    protected override void OnModelCreating(ModelBuilder modelBuilder)
    {
        modelBuilder.Entity<AuditLog>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasIndex(e => e.CreatedAt, "IX_AuditLogs_CreatedAt");

            entity.Property(e => e.Action).HasMaxLength(100);
            entity.Property(e => e.CreatedAt)
                .HasPrecision(0)
                .HasDefaultValueSql("(sysdatetime())");
            entity.Property(e => e.Details).HasMaxLength(1000);
            entity.Property(e => e.EntityId).HasMaxLength(50);
            entity.Property(e => e.EntityName).HasMaxLength(100);

            entity.HasOne(d => d.Employee).WithMany(p => p.AuditLogs)
                .HasForeignKey(d => d.EmployeeId)
                .HasConstraintName("FK_AuditLogs_Employees");
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<CafeTable>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasKey(e => e.TableId);

            entity.HasIndex(e => e.TableName, "UQ_CafeTables_TableName").IsUnique();

            entity.Property(e => e.Capacity).HasDefaultValue(4);
            entity.Property(e => e.IsActive).HasDefaultValue(true);
            entity.Property(e => e.TableName).HasMaxLength(50);
            entity.Property(e => e.TableStatus)
                .HasMaxLength(20)
                .HasDefaultValue("Available");
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<Category>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasIndex(e => e.CategoryName, "UQ_Categories_CategoryName").IsUnique();

            entity.Property(e => e.CategoryName).HasMaxLength(100);
            entity.Property(e => e.Description).HasMaxLength(250);
            entity.Property(e => e.IsActive).HasDefaultValue(true);
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<Employee>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasIndex(e => e.Username, "UQ_Employees_Username").IsUnique();

            entity.Property(e => e.CreatedAt)
                .HasPrecision(0)
                .HasDefaultValueSql("(sysdatetime())");
            entity.Property(e => e.Email).HasMaxLength(150);
            entity.Property(e => e.FullName).HasMaxLength(120);
            entity.Property(e => e.IsActive).HasDefaultValue(true);
            entity.Property(e => e.PasswordHash).HasMaxLength(500);
            entity.Property(e => e.Phone).HasMaxLength(20);
            entity.Property(e => e.UpdatedAt).HasPrecision(0);
            entity.Property(e => e.Username).HasMaxLength(50);

            entity.HasOne(d => d.Role).WithMany(p => p.Employees)
                .HasForeignKey(d => d.RoleId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.ClientSetNull)
                .HasConstraintName("FK_Employees_Roles");
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<Ingredient>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasIndex(e => e.IngredientName, "UQ_Ingredients_IngredientName").IsUnique();

            entity.Property(e => e.CurrentStock).HasColumnType("decimal(12, 3)");
            entity.Property(e => e.IngredientName).HasMaxLength(120);
            entity.Property(e => e.IsActive).HasDefaultValue(true);
            entity.Property(e => e.MinimumStock).HasColumnType("decimal(12, 3)");
            entity.Property(e => e.Unit).HasMaxLength(30);
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<InventoryTransaction>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasIndex(e => new { e.IngredientId, e.CreatedAt }, "IX_InventoryTransactions_Ingredient_Date");

            entity.Property(e => e.CreatedAt)
                .HasPrecision(0)
                .HasDefaultValueSql("(sysdatetime())");
            entity.Property(e => e.Note).HasMaxLength(300);
            entity.Property(e => e.QuantityChange).HasColumnType("decimal(12, 3)");
            entity.Property(e => e.ReferenceType).HasMaxLength(40);
            entity.Property(e => e.TransactionType).HasMaxLength(30);

            entity.HasOne(d => d.Employee).WithMany(p => p.InventoryTransactions)
                .HasForeignKey(d => d.EmployeeId)
                .HasConstraintName("FK_InventoryTransactions_Employees");

            entity.HasOne(d => d.Ingredient).WithMany(p => p.InventoryTransactions)
                .HasForeignKey(d => d.IngredientId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.ClientSetNull)
                .HasConstraintName("FK_InventoryTransactions_Ingredients");
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<Order>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasIndex(e => new { e.CreatedAt, e.OrderStatus }, "IX_Orders_CreatedAt_Status");

            entity.HasIndex(e => e.OrderCode, "UQ_Orders_OrderCode").IsUnique();

            entity.Property(e => e.CompletedAt).HasPrecision(0);
            entity.Property(e => e.CreatedAt)
                .HasPrecision(0)
                .HasDefaultValueSql("(sysdatetime())");
            entity.Property(e => e.DiscountAmount).HasColumnType("decimal(14, 2)");
            entity.Property(e => e.Note).HasMaxLength(500);
            entity.Property(e => e.OrderCode).HasMaxLength(30);
            entity.Property(e => e.OrderStatus)
                .HasMaxLength(25)
                .HasDefaultValue("New");
            entity.Property(e => e.OrderType)
                .HasMaxLength(20)
                .HasDefaultValue("DineIn");
            entity.Property(e => e.Subtotal).HasColumnType("decimal(14, 2)");
            entity.Property(e => e.TotalAmount).HasColumnType("decimal(14, 2)");

            entity.HasOne(d => d.CreatedByNavigation).WithMany(p => p.Orders)
                .HasForeignKey(d => d.CreatedBy)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.ClientSetNull)
                .HasConstraintName("FK_Orders_Employees");

            entity.HasOne(d => d.Table).WithMany(p => p.Orders)
                .HasForeignKey(d => d.TableId)
                .HasConstraintName("FK_Orders_CafeTables");
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<OrderItem>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasIndex(e => e.OrderId, "IX_OrderItems_OrderId");

            entity.Property(e => e.LineTotal).HasColumnType("decimal(14, 2)");
            entity.Property(e => e.Note).HasMaxLength(300);
            entity.Property(e => e.PreparationStatus)
                .HasMaxLength(20)
                .HasDefaultValue("Queued");
            entity.Property(e => e.ProductNameSnapshot).HasMaxLength(150);
            entity.Property(e => e.SizeNameSnapshot).HasMaxLength(30);
            entity.Property(e => e.UnitPrice).HasColumnType("decimal(12, 2)");

            entity.HasOne(d => d.Order).WithMany(p => p.OrderItems)
                .HasForeignKey(d => d.OrderId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.ClientSetNull)
                .HasConstraintName("FK_OrderItems_Orders");

            entity.HasOne(d => d.ProductSize).WithMany(p => p.OrderItems)
                .HasForeignKey(d => d.ProductSizeId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.ClientSetNull)
                .HasConstraintName("FK_OrderItems_ProductSizes");
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<OrderItemTopping>(entity =>
        {
            entity.Property(e => e.ExtraPrice).HasColumnType("decimal(12, 2)");
            entity.Property(e => e.Quantity).HasDefaultValue(1);
            entity.Property(e => e.ToppingNameSnapshot).HasMaxLength(100);

            entity.HasOne(d => d.OrderItem).WithMany(p => p.OrderItemToppings)
                .HasForeignKey(d => d.OrderItemId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.ClientSetNull)
                .HasConstraintName("FK_OrderItemToppings_OrderItems");

            entity.HasOne(d => d.Topping).WithMany(p => p.OrderItemToppings)
                .HasForeignKey(d => d.ToppingId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.ClientSetNull)
                .HasConstraintName("FK_OrderItemToppings_Toppings");
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<OrderStatusHistory>(entity =>
        {
            entity.ToTable("OrderStatusHistory");

            entity.Property(e => e.ChangedAt)
                .HasPrecision(0)
                .HasDefaultValueSql("(sysdatetime())");
            entity.Property(e => e.NewStatus).HasMaxLength(25);
            entity.Property(e => e.Note).HasMaxLength(300);
            entity.Property(e => e.OldStatus).HasMaxLength(25);

            entity.HasOne(d => d.ChangedByNavigation).WithMany(p => p.OrderStatusHistories)
                .HasForeignKey(d => d.ChangedBy)
                .HasConstraintName("FK_OrderStatusHistory_Employees");

            entity.HasOne(d => d.Order).WithMany(p => p.OrderStatusHistories)
                .HasForeignKey(d => d.OrderId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.ClientSetNull)
                .HasConstraintName("FK_OrderStatusHistory_Orders");
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<OrderVoucher>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasKey(e => new { e.OrderId, e.VoucherId });

            entity.Property(e => e.DiscountApplied).HasColumnType("decimal(12, 2)");

            entity.HasOne(d => d.Order).WithMany(p => p.OrderVouchers)
                .HasForeignKey(d => d.OrderId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.ClientSetNull)
                .HasConstraintName("FK_OrderVouchers_Orders");

            entity.HasOne(d => d.Voucher).WithMany(p => p.OrderVouchers)
                .HasForeignKey(d => d.VoucherId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.ClientSetNull)
                .HasConstraintName("FK_OrderVouchers_Vouchers");
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<Payment>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasIndex(e => new { e.OrderId, e.PaymentStatus }, "IX_Payments_OrderId_Status");

            entity.Property(e => e.Amount).HasColumnType("decimal(14, 2)");
            entity.Property(e => e.CreatedAt)
                .HasPrecision(0)
                .HasDefaultValueSql("(sysdatetime())");
            entity.Property(e => e.Note).HasMaxLength(300);
            entity.Property(e => e.PaidAt).HasPrecision(0);
            entity.Property(e => e.PaymentMethod).HasMaxLength(20);
            entity.Property(e => e.PaymentStatus)
                .HasMaxLength(20)
                .HasDefaultValue("Pending");
            entity.Property(e => e.TransactionRef).HasMaxLength(120);

            entity.HasOne(d => d.Order).WithMany(p => p.Payments)
                .HasForeignKey(d => d.OrderId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.ClientSetNull)
                .HasConstraintName("FK_Payments_Orders");

            entity.HasOne(d => d.ProcessedByNavigation).WithMany(p => p.Payments)
                .HasForeignKey(d => d.ProcessedBy)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.ClientSetNull)
                .HasConstraintName("FK_Payments_Employees");
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<Product>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasIndex(e => e.CategoryId, "IX_Products_CategoryId");

            entity.Property(e => e.CreatedAt)
                .HasPrecision(0)
                .HasDefaultValueSql("(sysdatetime())");
            entity.Property(e => e.Description).HasMaxLength(500);
            entity.Property(e => e.ImageUrl).HasMaxLength(500);
            entity.Property(e => e.IsAvailable).HasDefaultValue(true);
            entity.Property(e => e.ProductName).HasMaxLength(150);

            entity.HasOne(d => d.Category).WithMany(p => p.Products)
                .HasForeignKey(d => d.CategoryId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.ClientSetNull)
                .HasConstraintName("FK_Products_Categories");
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<ProductRecipe>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasKey(e => new { e.ProductSizeId, e.IngredientId });

            entity.Property(e => e.QuantityUsed).HasColumnType("decimal(12, 3)");

            entity.HasOne(d => d.Ingredient).WithMany(p => p.ProductRecipes)
                .HasForeignKey(d => d.IngredientId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.ClientSetNull)
                .HasConstraintName("FK_ProductRecipes_Ingredients");

            entity.HasOne(d => d.ProductSize).WithMany(p => p.ProductRecipes)
                .HasForeignKey(d => d.ProductSizeId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.ClientSetNull)
                .HasConstraintName("FK_ProductRecipes_ProductSizes");
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<ProductSize>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasIndex(e => new { e.ProductId, e.SizeName }, "UQ_ProductSizes_Product_Size").IsUnique();

            entity.Property(e => e.IsAvailable).HasDefaultValue(true);
            entity.Property(e => e.Price).HasColumnType("decimal(12, 2)");
            entity.Property(e => e.SizeName).HasMaxLength(30);

            entity.HasOne(d => d.Product).WithMany(p => p.ProductSizes)
                .HasForeignKey(d => d.ProductId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.ClientSetNull)
                .HasConstraintName("FK_ProductSizes_Products");
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<Role>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasIndex(e => e.RoleName, "UQ_Roles_RoleName").IsUnique();

            entity.Property(e => e.Description).HasMaxLength(250);
            entity.Property(e => e.RoleName).HasMaxLength(50);
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<StockReceipt>(entity =>
        {
            entity.Property(e => e.Note).HasMaxLength(500);
            entity.Property(e => e.ReceiptDate)
                .HasPrecision(0)
                .HasDefaultValueSql("(sysdatetime())");
            entity.Property(e => e.TotalCost).HasColumnType("decimal(14, 2)");

            entity.HasOne(d => d.ReceivedByNavigation).WithMany(p => p.StockReceipts)
                .HasForeignKey(d => d.ReceivedBy)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.ClientSetNull)
                .HasConstraintName("FK_StockReceipts_Employees");

            entity.HasOne(d => d.Supplier).WithMany(p => p.StockReceipts)
                .HasForeignKey(d => d.SupplierId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.ClientSetNull)
                .HasConstraintName("FK_StockReceipts_Suppliers");
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<StockReceiptDetail>(entity =>
        {
            entity.Property(e => e.Quantity).HasColumnType("decimal(12, 3)");
            entity.Property(e => e.UnitCost).HasColumnType("decimal(12, 2)");

            entity.HasOne(d => d.Ingredient).WithMany(p => p.StockReceiptDetails)
                .HasForeignKey(d => d.IngredientId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.ClientSetNull)
                .HasConstraintName("FK_StockReceiptDetails_Ingredients");

            entity.HasOne(d => d.StockReceipt).WithMany(p => p.StockReceiptDetails)
                .HasForeignKey(d => d.StockReceiptId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.ClientSetNull)
                .HasConstraintName("FK_StockReceiptDetails_Receipts");
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<Supplier>(entity =>
        {
            entity.Property(e => e.Address).HasMaxLength(300);
            entity.Property(e => e.Email).HasMaxLength(150);
            entity.Property(e => e.IsActive).HasDefaultValue(true);
            entity.Property(e => e.Phone).HasMaxLength(20);
            entity.Property(e => e.SupplierName).HasMaxLength(150);
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<TableReservation>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasKey(e => e.ReservationId);

            entity.Property(e => e.CustomerName).HasMaxLength(120);
            entity.Property(e => e.CustomerPhone).HasMaxLength(20);
            entity.Property(e => e.Note).HasMaxLength(300);
            entity.Property(e => e.ReservationAt).HasPrecision(0);
            entity.Property(e => e.ReservationStatus)
                .HasMaxLength(20)
                .HasDefaultValue("Booked");

            entity.HasOne(d => d.CreatedByNavigation).WithMany(p => p.TableReservations)
                .HasForeignKey(d => d.CreatedBy)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.ClientSetNull)
                .HasConstraintName("FK_TableReservations_Employees");

            entity.HasOne(d => d.Table).WithMany(p => p.TableReservations)
                .HasForeignKey(d => d.TableId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.ClientSetNull)
                .HasConstraintName("FK_TableReservations_Tables");
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<Topping>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasIndex(e => e.ToppingName, "UQ_Toppings_ToppingName").IsUnique();

            entity.Property(e => e.ExtraPrice).HasColumnType("decimal(12, 2)");
            entity.Property(e => e.IsAvailable).HasDefaultValue(true);
            entity.Property(e => e.ToppingName).HasMaxLength(100);
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<ToppingRecipe>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasKey(e => new { e.ToppingId, e.IngredientId });

            entity.Property(e => e.QuantityUsed).HasColumnType("decimal(12, 3)");

            entity.HasOne(d => d.Ingredient).WithMany(p => p.ToppingRecipes)
                .HasForeignKey(d => d.IngredientId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.ClientSetNull)
                .HasConstraintName("FK_ToppingRecipes_Ingredients");

            entity.HasOne(d => d.Topping).WithMany(p => p.ToppingRecipes)
                .HasForeignKey(d => d.ToppingId)
                .OnDelete(DeleteBehavior.ClientSetNull)
                .HasConstraintName("FK_ToppingRecipes_Toppings");
        });

        modelBuilder.Entity<Voucher>(entity =>
        {
            entity.HasIndex(e => e.VoucherCode, "UQ_Vouchers_VoucherCode").IsUnique();

            entity.Property(e => e.Description).HasMaxLength(250);
            entity.Property(e => e.DiscountType).HasMaxLength(10);
            entity.Property(e => e.DiscountValue).HasColumnType("decimal(12, 2)");
            entity.Property(e => e.EndAt).HasPrecision(0);
            entity.Property(e => e.IsActive).HasDefaultValue(true);
            entity.Property(e => e.MaximumDiscount).HasColumnType("decimal(12, 2)");
            entity.Property(e => e.MinimumOrder).HasColumnType("decimal(12, 2)");
            entity.Property(e => e.StartAt).HasPrecision(0);
            entity.Property(e => e.VoucherCode).HasMaxLength(40);
        });

        OnModelCreatingPartial(modelBuilder);
    }


    partial void OnModelCreatingPartial(ModelBuilder modelBuilder);
}

namespace CoffeeRuu.Models
{
    public class LowStockAlertItem
    {
        public int IngredientId { get; set; }

        public string IngredientName { get; set; } = "";

        public string Unit { get; set; } = "";

        public decimal CurrentStock { get; set; }

        public decimal MinimumStock { get; set; }

        public decimal ShortageQuantity =>
            Math.Max(0, MinimumStock - CurrentStock);

        public string StockStatus =>
            CurrentStock == 0
                ? "Hết hàng"
                : "Tồn kho thấp";
    }
}
